Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any homotopy module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 533. The projective resolution has length at most 491 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 533 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the homotopy structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 491. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
